code-mode-protocol/src/description.rs:30: - You may optionally start the tool input with a first-line pragma like `// @exec: {"yield_time_ms": 10000, "max_output_tokens": 1000}`.
code-mode-protocol/src/description.rs:31: - `yield_time_ms` asks `exec` to yield early if the script is still running. Defaults to {{ default_exec_yield_time_ms }} ms.
code-mode-protocol/src/description.rs:32: - `max_output_tokens` sets the token budget for direct `exec` results. Defaults to 10000 tokens.
code-mode-protocol/src/description.rs:33: - When the JS code is fully evaluated, the isolate's lifetime ends and unawaited promises are silently discarded.
code-mode-protocol/src/description.rs:34: 
code-mode-protocol/src/description.rs:35: - Global helpers:

ext/agent-message-board/src/extension.rs:142: /// Installs message-board lifecycle and tool contributions. The factory selects
ext/agent-message-board/src/extension.rs:143: /// a local or remote backend, or returns None when disabled. Configuration is
ext/agent-message-board/src/extension.rs:144: /// read at runtime startup, including resume; no board is created by installation.
ext/agent-message-board/src/extension.rs:145: /// The host supplies its shared namespace description and resolves the namespace
